everybody wrong, so when my family or the banker says my numbers don’t add up, I just think they’re too small‑minded to see what a breakthrough I’m making.\n- S036 (Decreased_Sleep): I’ve gotten used to sleeping maybe three hours a night, if that, because my head is so full of ideas and I feel so wired that I’ll just get up at two in the morning to work on spreadsheets or walk the fields with a flashlight instead of ever feeling tired.\n- S038 (Racing_Thoughts): My thoughts run so fast now that while I’m trying to figure out a fertilizer mix, I’m already planning three new workshops, thinking about buying another drone, remembering something someone said at the co‑op, and it all piles on at once so I can’t shut my brain off even when I want to.\n- S039 (Manic_Distractibility): delusions_and_hallucinations_occur_for_2_or_more_weeks_in_the_absence_of_a_major_mood_episode: Even during a spell a few months back when my energy dipped and I wasn’t tearing around the farm as much, I still heard that man’s voice at night and stayed convinced the seed companies and co‑op were watching my every move with the drones.\n- functional_impairment: I’ve let basic chores and paperwork pile up so badly that fields went unworked, bills went unpaid, and my family had to step in to stop me from signing a big equipment loan because they were afraid I was going to bankrupt the farm and tear the family apart.\n- duration: All of this—the big swings in my drive and mood along with these strange ideas and voices—has been building and going on since I pushed hard into the new tech expansion on the farm about a year and a bit ago.\n- psychotic_symptoms_duration: The fixed belief that the co‑op and seed companies are tracking me and the man’s voice guiding and warning me have insist on a medical evaluation, which led to psychiatric referral.\n- Chief Complaint: “I’m absolutely certain I’m seeing things more clearly than everyone else and that my ideas can’t be wrong, even when my family says they make no sense.”\n▸ Patient-specific Symptom Manifestations\n- S019 (Delusions): I know everyone keeps telling me it’s just not possible, but I’m convinced the seed companies and the co‑op board are secretly tracking everything I do with the drones because they want to steal my regenerative system and shut our farm down if I don’t play along with their plan.\n- S020 (Hallucinations): Out in the fields or lying in bed at night, I hear a man’s voice as clear as if he’s standing next to me, telling me which rows to plant and warning me that my family is trying to I’ve lost it.\n- S033 (Elevated_Irritable_Mood): For months at a time I swing between feeling on top of the world, talking a mile a minute and getting angry if anyone so much as questions a tiny part of my plan, and then snapping at my wife or the kids over nothing when they ask me to slow down or take a break.\n- S034 (Increased_Activity): Most days I’m buzzing from the minute I wake up, racing from the computer to the fields to the shed, starting ten different projects—rewriting our entire crop plan, rearranging the shop, sending long emails about new workshops—and I just can’t sit still long enough to finish any of them.\n- S035 (Inflated_Self_Esteem): I honestly feel like I’m the only one in this county who really understands where farming is headed, like I’ve been chosen to prove everybody wrong, so when my family or the banker says my numbers don’t add up, I just think they’re too small‑minded to see what a breakthrough I’m making.\n- S036 (Decreased_Sleep): I’ve gotten used thinking about buying another drone, remembering something someone said at the co‑op, and it all piles on at once so I can’t shut my brain off even when I want to.\n- S039 (Manic_Distractibility): Subtypes: Intrusive Memories, Distressing Dreams, Dissociative Reactions (Flashbacks), Psychological Distress at Cues, Physiological Reactions to Cues\n[S070] Trauma_Avoidance: Persistent avoidance of stimuli associated with the traumatic event.  Subtypes: Internal Avoidance, External Avoidance\n[S071] Trauma_Related_Amnesia: Inability to remember an important aspect of the traumatic event (typically due to dissociative amnesia and not to other factors like head injury or alcohol).  Subtypes: Fragmented Memory, Generalized Amnesia\n[S072] Negative_Beliefs: Persistent and exaggerated negative beliefs or expectations about oneself, others, or the world. Subtypes: Self-Blame, Blaming Others\n[S074] Persistent_Negative_Emotional_State: Persistent negative emotional state (e.g., fear, horror, anger, guilt, or shame).  Subtypes: Persistent Fear, Persistent Horror, Persistent Anger, Persistent Guilt, Persistent Shame\n[S075] Social_Detachment: Feelings of detachment or estrangement from others.  Subtypes: Emotional Detachment, Social Isolation, Difficulty with Intimacy, Loss of Belonging\n[S076] Inability_Positive_Emotions: Persistent inability to experience positive emotions (e.g., inability to experience happiness, satisfaction, or loving feelings).  Subtypes: Anhedonia for Happiness, Lack of Satisfaction, Inability to Feel Love, Emotional Numbness\n[S077] Irritability_Aggression: Irritable behavior and angry outbursts (with little or no provocation) typically expressed as verbal or physical aggression.  Subtypes: Impulsive Anger, Verbal Aggression, Physical Aggression, Chronic Irritability\n[S078] Reckless_Self_Destructive_Behavior: Reckless or self-destructive behavior pattern that endangers one's safety or health.
